# Fullseye — ViEW2026

Combiner simulation physique et traitement d'images avec l'IA, et vérifier le résultat par rapport à la vérité terrain.

**Présentation : jeudi 3 décembre 2026, 14 h 40–15 h 50 JST, session interactive 2, IS2-19** (Pacifico Yokohama)

Touchez une vignette pour ouvrir la vidéo ou la figure (▶ = animé).

## Essayez par vous-même

Manipulez vous-même les figures de l'article, même sur téléphone. Changez de méthode avec les onglets et faites pivoter les vues 3-D en les faisant glisser.

- **[CAO contre nuage de points scanné](../viewers/poc_cad_scan_deviation.html?lang=fr)**: Le recalage absorbe les défauts : une pièce de valeur vraie 1.2 µm montre un creux de 121 µm qui n'existe pas.
- **[Changement de terrain par LiDAR](../viewers/poc_lidar_terrain_change.html?lang=fr)**: Une simple soustraction fait paraître tout le bloc inchangé 0.183 m plus bas. Une fois corrigé, seul le vrai changement reste.
- **[Suivi par modèle](../viewers/poc_template_tracking.html?lang=fr)**: Même après avoir basculé sur un objet qui lui ressemble, le suivi continue de signaler « trouvé ».
- **[Profondeur par thermographie pulsée](../viewers/poc_thermography_ndt.html?lang=fr)**: Raccourcir la fenêtre d'ajustement de 25 s à 4 s ramène la pire erreur de profondeur des petits défauts de +627% à ±14%. La cause était le choix de la fenêtre, pas la physique.
- **[Tapis de Talbot et images X par réseaux](../viewers/poc_grating_interferometry.html?lang=fr)**: En absorption, le rapport contraste/bruit de la fissure n'est que de 1.34 ; en champ sombre il monte à 13.5, et en tournant le réseau on lit même la direction des fibres.
- **[Creux de quelques µm dans l'image-miroir de franges](../viewers/poc_phase_measuring_deflectometry.html?lang=fr)**: Dans une photo, le signal vaut 0.17 fois le bruit et rien n'apparaît ; dans la carte de courbure issue de l'image-miroir de franges, un creux de 1 µm dépasse le bruit de 57 fois.
- **[À quel point la surface d'un astéroïde se courbe](../viewers/poc_asteroid_curvature.html?lang=fr)**: La courbure est calculée à partir des points d'un astéroïde réel ; crêtes et bords de blocs apparaissent en couleur. La règle a d'abord été vérifiée sur une sphère dont la réponse est connue.
- **[Empilement de mise au point](../viewers/poc_focus_stacking.html?lang=fr)**: Augmenter le nombre d’ouverture rapproche l’image empilée de la texture vraie
- **[Marches en interférométrie en lumière blanche](../viewers/poc_interferometry_step.html?lang=fr)**: Le bruit élargit l’erreur de marche en interférométrie en lumière blanche
- **[Suppression des reflets spéculaires par polarisation](../viewers/poc_polarization_specular.html?lang=fr)**: La polarisation retire les reflets spéculaires ; le reste varie avec l’incidence et atteint son minimum près de l’angle de Brewster (balayage sur une scène synthétique à franges)
- **[Contraintes en photoélasticimétrie](../viewers/poc_photoelasticity.html?lang=fr)**: Tourner l’analyseur déplace les franges photoélastiques et modifie l’intensité moyenne dans le disque (sur 0–135°)
- **[Œil composé de la mouche](../viewers/poc_compound_eye.html?lang=fr)**: Augmenter le SNR par vue rapproche l’image de l’œil composé de l’image sans bruit
- **[Vides dans la couche de jonction](../viewers/poc_ct_void_morphology.html?lang=fr)**: Relever le seuil étend les vides segmentés et augmente leur fraction volumique
- **[Contours actifs](../viewers/poc_active_contours.html?lang=fr)**: Les itérations rapprochent le contour GVF de sa forme finale

Certaines descriptions de PoC sont en anglais.

<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_industrial_oct/01_real_paint_bscans.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_industrial_oct/01_real_paint_bscans.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_industrial_oct/story.en.gif" alt="We want to see the layers of paint and multilayer film in depth without cutting them. Layers too thin to separate are reported as unmeasurable instead of guessed." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Épaisseur des couches de peinture sans découpe (OCT industrielle)</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_hyperspectral_foreign_matter/04_real_pork_belly.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_hyperspectral_foreign_matter/01_real_polymer_flakes.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_hyperspectral_foreign_matter/story.en.gif" alt="Foreign matter the same colour as meat or kernels cannot be told apart by a colour camera. Classifying public real images as spectral data detected debris on pork belly with 1.1 % false alarms. With fewer bands the false alarms grew, so a band set that sufficed on synthetic data did not suffice on real data." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Trouver des corps étrangers couleur viande par imagerie spectrale</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_thin_film_reflectometry/01_real_micrograph_thickness.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_thin_film_reflectometry/01_real_micrograph_thickness.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_thin_film_reflectometry/story.en.gif" alt="Oxide thickness on silicon can be read from interference fringes and colours in reflected light. From a real micrograph published by other researchers, a per-pixel thickness map was drawn, centred on 87.4 nm. It stays below the reference value, and the camera's colour sensitivity is unknown." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Épaisseur d'oxyde cartographiée d'après les couleurs d'une micrographie</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_telecentric_metrology/08_real_alignment_plate.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_telecentric_metrology/08_real_alignment_plate.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_telecentric_metrology/story.en.gif" alt="Measured in one image, a stepped part looks larger where it is nearer, so an ordinary lens misreads its dimensions. On the published real images from a telecentric lens, the magnification matched the paper within 0.05 %. In the synthetic chapter, telecentric magnification barely moves with object height." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Grandissement vérifié à 0.05 % sur des images télécentriques publiques</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_virtual_sem_kv_cd_bse/05_sem_same_part_3kv_3detectors.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_virtual_sem_kv_cd_bse/05_sem_same_part_3kv_3detectors.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_virtual_sem_kv_cd_bse/story.en.gif" alt="An SEM image is not a photo of the surface but a tally of electrons escaping after scattering inside the solid. A model that tracks each electron was checked against measured backscatter data for 7 elements. On the same line, the beam energy decides which sidewall height is read as the linewidth." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>MEB virtuel : la tension change l'image et la largeur de ligne</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_cad_scan_deviation/14_align_orbit.gif"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_cad_scan_deviation/01_scene.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_cad_scan_deviation/story.en.gif" alt="Aligning a scan to its design can absorb a defect or move it elsewhere. Known dents and warps were added to a part; even the defect-free control produced 66.5 square mm of false rejected area. Rigid-like deviations shrink, while nearest points jump across edges and create a fictitious dent." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>L'alignement masque les défauts : CAO contre scan</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_good_sample_variation_model/04_visa_explanation.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_good_sample_variation_model/01_band_width_alignment.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_good_sample_variation_model/story.en.gif" alt="A classic inspection learns the usual per-pixel variation from good parts and flags what falls outside. Here the band is set by a false-alarm rate declared in advance and scored on 200 public real images. Alignment mattered more than the number of good parts, and the declared rate was not kept." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Défauts d'après la variabilité des bonnes pièces : le taux de fausses alarmes annoncé ne tient pas sur images réelles</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_surface_defect_classifiers/01_defect_patches.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_surface_defect_classifiers/01_defect_patches.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_surface_defect_classifiers/story.en.gif" alt="Small pieces of ground metal were drawn with scratches, dents and stains by physics, and sorted with trained classifiers. Stained pixels were painted with an overlap of 0.915 by a classifier learned from normal pixels. Flaws that run with the grinding marks are easily missed, and unknown holes are rejected." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Trier les défauts de surface métallique par classifieurs appris : les rayures le long des stries de meulage sont manquées trois fois sur quatre, les piqûres inconnues rejetées à 95 %</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ai_built_inspection_scored/01_crack_real.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ai_built_inspection_scored/01_crack_real.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ai_built_inspection_scored/story.en.gif" alt="We gave an AI a task in natural language and let it assemble the inspection recipe. It was scored on 400 public crack images against the labels from their source, and each failure was sorted into tool, MCP or model. Cloud LLMs beat chance but did not reach the existing recipes." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Noter sur données réelles des inspections construites par une IA</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_bottle_pattern_inverse/01_checker_through_bottle.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_bottle_pattern_inverse/01_checker_through_bottle.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_bottle_pattern_inverse/story.en.gif" alt="A transparent bottle distorts the stripes behind it. For each pixel we find where the background is seen, fit the bottle geometry and indices backwards, and score 5 fill states against the true values of the simulation. Quantities that are inseparable and errors that stay undetectable are reported too." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Relire une bouteille en verre à partir de rayures d'arrière-plan (simulation) : reconstruction sous hypothèses partagées, ce qui ne se sépare ni ne se détecte</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_tum_rgbd_slam/02_trajectory.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_tum_rgbd_slam/02_trajectory.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_tum_rgbd_slam/story.en.gif" alt="On public depth images of a desk, frame-to-frame alignment was checked against motion-capture truth. Error fell to 0.42 of no alignment, yet it was no better than assuming steady speed. Small-window plane residuals grew with distance, but quantisation and window size blur whether the law is a square." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Alignement image à image noté sur des données RGB-D publiques : l'erreur tombe à 0,42 fois, à peu près au niveau de l'hypothèse de vitesse constante</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_walnut_cbct/07_geometry_mistakes.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_walnut_cbct/07_geometry_mistakes.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_walnut_cbct/story.en.gif" alt="A walnut was really scanned in a cone beam, and the data were reconstructed with FDK and iterative methods. Using 300 projections and scoring against the reference from its authors, the result that ignores the vertical shift ends up much more distant. But the residual after reprojection cannot reveal the gap." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>CT mesuré : ignorer le décalage vertical éloigne 2,6 fois de la référence, mais le résidu ne le voit pas</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_3d_sensor_modalities/01_map.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_3d_sensor_modalities/01_map.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_3d_sensor_modalities/story.en.gif" alt="The same part was measured by several depth-sensing methods in matte, glossy metal, translucent and black materials. Shape errors were counted with a common 0.1 mm yardstick and compared with the dimension table. Which material breaks which method, and where, differs from method to method." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Une pièce, cinq méthodes de mesure 3-D</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_equipment_selection_map/02_equipment_at_a_glance.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_equipment_selection_map/02_equipment_at_a_glance.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_equipment_selection_map/story.en.gif" alt="We pre-tested visual inspection equipment on the same flaw. It is a synthetic test, not the performance of real equipment." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Carte de choix du matériel : les mêmes défauts vus par 60 combinaisons éclairage, caméra, objectif</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_stereo_multiscanline/02_failure_map.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_stereo_multiscanline/02_failure_map.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_stereo_multiscanline/story.en.gif" alt="Using a real pair of stereo images, we compute disparity, distance, surfaces and even the pose of a circle. Where it breaks is shown on a map." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Disparité, surface et pose de cercle en stéréo réelle</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_defect_floor/03_defect_floor_explained.mp4"><img src="../thumbs/poc_real_defect_floor.jpg" alt="Bruit égal, mais les défauts faibles se cachent sur fond réel" loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"><b>&#9654;</b><span>Bruit égal, mais les défauts faibles se cachent sur fond réel</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_sam_hybrid_bonding_voids/03_amplitude_vs_phase.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_sam_hybrid_bonding_voids/03_amplitude_vs_phase.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_sam_hybrid_bonding_voids/story.en.gif" alt="Small voids in the bonding plane of stacked chips are searched for with a scanning acoustic microscope. Every void of 16 um or more appeared inverted and bright in the echo-phase image. In the brightness image many small voids hide among good pixels." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Trouver les vides du plan de collage par la phase de l'écho ultrasonore</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_photoelasticity/03_load_and_isoclinics.mp4"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_photoelasticity/01_polariscope.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_photoelasticity/story.en.gif" alt="Viewed through polarisers, a loaded transparent plastic shows its internal stress as fringes. Stress in a squeezed disc was recovered from polarisation images, including pixels where the fringe order exceeds 0.5 and the phase wraps. Most errors left after unwrapping came from the sign ambiguity, not the wrapping." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>Les contraintes d'un disque comprimé en franges (photoélasticité)</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_mixed_case_depalletizing/08_depal_robot_video.mp4"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_mixed_case_depalletizing/01_overview_same_pattern.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_mixed_case_depalletizing/story.en.gif" alt="Packed cartons with identical prints are told apart from overhead depth and colour, then unloaded. A depth step splits none, and whether depth dips or dark colour lines win flips across 28 flap-seam builds. With identical prints, deep grooves favour colour; only wide, untaped cuts over shallow grooves favour depth." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>Cartons mélangés : marche de profondeur, rainure en V ou couleur</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_code_matching_breakdown/05_highlight_sweep.gif"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_code_matching_breakdown/01_breakdown_occlusion.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_code_matching_breakdown/story.en.gif" alt="Normalised cross-correlation, the usual template matcher, fails when part of the target is hidden or glare washes it out. Matchers comparing the sign of brightness changes were tested on 40 templates from public real photos. They withstand occlusion far better, yet every method stays weak to rotation." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>Appariement par codes résistant aux reflets et à l'occultation</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_caltab_chain_truth/01_caltab_chain.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_caltab_chain_truth/01_caltab_chain.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_caltab_chain_truth/story.en.gif" alt="A calibration plate with circular marks is imaged and taken from point detection to camera calibration, then scored against the true camera. Even without noise the focal length is off by 0.45 %. That floor is set by the centroid of the binarised marks." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Mire à pastilles de bout en bout : fx dévie de 0,45 % même sans bruit, et le barycentre binaire fixe ce plancher</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_tdi_linescan/01_tdi_stages_defects.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_tdi_linescan/01_tdi_stages_defects.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_tdi_linescan/story.en.gif" alt="Moving material such as film or steel strip is imaged with a line sensor. On a dark line, stacking 64 stages of exposure lifts faint scratches and inclusions out of the texture. If transport and capture drift out of sync, the stacked image smears." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Empiler les étages TDI fait ressortir les défauts faibles sur une ligne sombre</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_itof_multifreq/12_public_raw_unwrap.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_itof_multifreq/01_warehouse_unwrap.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_itof_multifreq/story.en.gif" alt="An indirect ToF camera measures distance from the phase delay of modulated light. Combining three frequencies keeps the distance unambiguous out to 18.7 m. Multipath and object edges make the distance jump without any warning." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Où la profondeur ToF indirecte se brise</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_radiograph_porosity/02_map_detections.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_radiograph_porosity/02_map_detections.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_radiograph_porosity/story.en.gif" alt="Miscounting pores in a weld radiograph leads to the wrong weld grade. A fixed cut-off also counts the edge of the weld cap and reports 124 blobs. Estimating the background before detection keeps only real pores, and far fewer images are graded wrongly." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Pores sur radiographie de soudure : un seuil fixe prend aussi le bord du cordon</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_fourier_ptychography/01_usaf_4x_fp_20x.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_fourier_ptychography/01_usaf_4x_fp_20x.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_fourier_ptychography/story.en.gif" alt="A low-magnification objective sees a wide field but misses fine detail. Combining images lit from different angles in frequency space resolved detail as fine as a 20x image over the same wide field. When the illumination overlap is too small to converge, no answer is returned." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Résolution 20x dans un champ 4x (ptychographie de Fourier)</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_illumination_cone_vs_na/02_works_by_cone.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_illumination_cone_vs_na/02_works_by_cone.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_illumination_cone_vs_na/story.en.gif" alt="On near-mirror metal parts, shallow dents and scratches on a hairline finish show or vanish with the width of the cone of light more than with the lamp type. With a cone half-angle of 4°, the hairline streaks disappear and only the scratch stays dark. A single dent can appear or disappear as the cone opens." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Le cône de lumière décide si creux et rayures sont visibles</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_simcal_telecentric/02_real_model_residuals.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_simcal_telecentric/02_real_model_residuals.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_simcal_telecentric/story.en.gif" alt="An imaging model was fitted to a published real image of a tilted alignment plate seen through a telecentric lens. The magnification came out at 252.14 px/mm, close to the paper's value. Places where the model does not fit are kept and drawn as residual needles on the image." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Étalonnage sur une image télécentrique réelle, écarts montrés</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_lidar_terrain_change/13_real_dod.png"><img src="../thumbs/poc_lidar_terrain_change.jpg" alt="LiDAR aéroporté à deux dates : sans retirer le décalage, tout semble creusé" loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"><span>LiDAR aéroporté à deux dates : sans retirer le décalage, tout semble creusé</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_stereo_depth/01_scene.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_stereo_depth/01_scene.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_stereo_depth/story.en.gif" alt="A public real stereo photograph with true disparity is used to check depth measurement. Setting the disparity search below the true maximum loses the whole foreground, with 95.06 % of pixels wrong. When converting to distance, ignoring a single calibration value bends the shape." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Stéréo réelle : une plage de disparité trop courte perd tout le premier plan</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_sem_tem_same_particle/08_tem_focus_sweep.gif"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_sem_tem_same_particle/02_same_specimen_sem_tem.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_sem_tem_same_particle/story.en.gif" alt="The same nanoparticles are sized in both a scanning and a transmission electron image. Even for a 52 nm particle, the reading moved by nanometres with the imaging mode and cut-off level. Scored against a shared truth, both biases follow simple formulas for edge blur and defocus." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>Mêmes particules, tailles différentes de quelques nm entre MEB et MET</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_tem_ctf_thon_rings/12_real_empiar_10028.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_tem_ctf_thon_rings/02_thon_rings_four_defoci.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_tem_ctf_thon_rings/story.en.gif" alt="In a transmission electron microscope, defocus decides whether fine detail looks bright or dark. Estimating defocus from 3 public real micrographs closely matched the authors' own software. Sweeping focus moves the concentric Thon rings and flips detail near true focus." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Défocalisation estimée sur images MET réelles, à 0.2 % des auteurs</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_optics_drawings/06_setup_ring_coaxial.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_optics_drawings/06_setup_ring_coaxial.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_optics_drawings/story.en.gif" alt="In meetings about inspection systems, the lens layout and the setup drawing are the common language. The optical calculation now draws them directly, up to ISO 10110 element drawings. Each written file is read back, and its lines and text are scored against the calculation." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Plans d'installation et de lentilles issus directement du calcul optique</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_crack_width_timeseries/11_series_video.gif"><img src="../thumbs/poc_crack_width_timeseries.jpg" alt="Croissance de fissure : compter les pixels surestime la vitesse de 2.5 fois" loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"><b>&#9654;</b><span>Croissance de fissure : compter les pixels surestime la vitesse de 2.5 fois</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_moire_alignment/01_marks.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_moire_alignment/02_one_nm_steps.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_moire_alignment/story.en.gif" alt="Lithography must overlay a mask and a wafer to within nanometres. Overlaying two gratings too fine for the camera to see produces 102 µm fringes that magnify the shift. Reading the fringe position recovers shifts smaller than a nanometre." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Deux réseaux invisibles : les franges de moiré lisent des décalages nanométriques</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_laser_line_speckle_floor/01_line.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_laser_line_speckle_floor/01_line.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_laser_line_speckle_floor/story.en.gif" alt="When a laser line measures height, speckle, the grainy pattern of interfering light, breaks up the line. Reading position by the centroid still leaves an error of 6.4 µm, close to the floor the formula predicts. Averaging frames of a static part does not help; only moving the part lowers it." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Le speckle laser fixe le plancher de précision en hauteur de la triangulation laser</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_bead_scan_angle/02_frames.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_bead_scan_angle/02_frames.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weld_bead_scan_angle/story.en.gif" alt="When a laser line traces a weld bead, a steeper camera angle measures height more finely. Even at 28 degrees, part of the undercut groove falls into its own shadow and its depth reads shallow. The shadowed span still looks measured, which makes the loss easy to miss." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><span>Coupe laser d'un cordon : un angle plus raide cache le caniveau dans sa propre ombre</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_am_thermal_to_ct/07_ct_hole_crown.png"><img src="../thumbs/poc_am_thermal_to_ct.jpg" alt="Rugosité des faces orientées vers le bas sur un vrai scanner d'une pièce de fabrication additive" loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"><span>Rugosité des faces orientées vers le bas sur un vrai scanner d'une pièce de fabrication additive</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_thermography_ndt/07_window_explained.mp4"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_thermography_ndt/01_depth_table.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_thermography_ndt/story.en.gif" alt="A flash warms the surface, and the cooling thermal images reveal how deep a hidden delamination lies. For a small, shallow defect, cutting the fitting window to 4 s restored a correct depth. The error came not from heat spreading sideways but from the choice of window." loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>Profondeur de délaminage par thermographie : la fenêtre temporelle change la réponse</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_interferometry_step/09_csi_scan_explained.mp4"><img src="../thumbs/poc_interferometry_step.jpg" alt="Marches nanométriques par interférométrie en lumière blanche" loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"><b>&#9654;</b><span>Marches nanométriques par interférométrie en lumière blanche</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_glyph_typo_detection/01_sign_before_after.png"><img src="../thumbs/poc_glyph_typo_detection.jpg" alt="Trouver et corriger les coquilles d'un panneau sans le lire" loading="lazy" width="320" height="320"><span>Trouver et corriger les coquilles d'un panneau sans le lire</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weed_rice_ratio/05_real_examples.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weed_rice_ratio/05_real_examples.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_weed_rice_ratio/story.en.gif" alt="Aerial photos of rice paddies are graded by how much weedy rice is mixed into the crop. ## Ce qu'il permet de faire

101 sujets, chacun avec une page d'explication (utilisation et limites) et des liens vers des exemples exécutables.

<div class="vl" markdown="1">

**Trouver**

- Découper des régions, les sélectionner et les compter: [explication](../../capabilities/blob-and-region.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_cell_counting](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_cell_counting.py) · [poc_particle_sizing](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_particle_sizing.py) · [poc_real_coin_metrology](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_coin_metrology.py) · [poc_dense_bubble_count](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_dense_bubble_count.py)
- Séparer culture et adventices dans une image RVB de champ par la couleur et la texture, puis classer le taux de couverture des adventices selon des seuils déclarés à l'avance (sans réseau appris): [explication](../../capabilities/crop-weed-cover-grade.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_weed_rice_ratio](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_weed_rice_ratio.py)
- Séparer épaisseur et matériau avec deux énergies de rayons X (bi-énergie et comptage de photons : spectre, fenêtres d'énergie, décomposition en base, numéro atomique effectif): [explication](../../capabilities/dual-energy-xray-material-discrimination.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_dual_energy_xray](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_dual_energy_xray.py)
- Corriger le texte contenu dans une image par rapport à la chaîne attendue: [explication](../../capabilities/fix-text-in-images.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [fix_text_in_image](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/fix_text_in_image.py) · [poc_glyph_typo_detection](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_glyph_typo_detection.py)
- Signaler ce qui diffère de pièces bonnes alignées (modèle de variation par pixel, taux de fausses alarmes déclaré): [explication](../../capabilities/good-sample-variation-model.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_good_sample_variation_model](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_good_sample_variation_model.py) · [anomaly_eval_metrics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/anomaly_eval_metrics.py)
- Prétraitement pour l'OCR (correction de l'inclinaison, binarisation locale, découpage des lignes): [explication](../../capabilities/ocr-preprocessing.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [gallery2d_segmentation](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/gallery2d_segmentation.py) · [poc_matrix_code_reading](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_matrix_code_reading.py) · [gallery2d_region](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/gallery2d_region.py)
- Apprendre des seules bonnes pièces et désigner les patchs qui s'en écartent (caractéristiques classiques : loi normale par position ou banque mémoire, point de fonctionnement à rappel garanti): [explication](../../capabilities/patch-feature-anomaly-detection.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_patch_anomaly](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_patch_anomaly.py) · [anomaly_eval_metrics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/anomaly_eval_metrics.py)
- Distinguer, par la PSF de diffusion des porteurs et le type de défaut, pourquoi une rainure fine n'est plus visible sur une image PL (inspection PL/EL des plaquettes de Si et des cellules solaires): [explication](../../capabilities/pl-carrier-diffusion-visibility.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_si_wafer_pl_defect_visibility](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_si_wafer_pl_defect_visibility.py)
- Détecter de petites cibles ponctuelles et en déterminer la position au niveau sous-pixel: [explication](../../capabilities/point-target-detection.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_search_sweep_width](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_search_sweep_width.py) · [poc_astro_photometry](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_astro_photometry.py)
- Appariement de gabarits robuste à l'éclairage inégal, aux reflets et aux occlusions (comparer le sens de la variation de luminosité comme un code, refuser les gabarits indéterminés): [explication](../../capabilities/robust-code-matching.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_code_matching_breakdown](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_code_matching_breakdown.py)
- Détecter les vides d'un collage hybride par la phase de l'écho ultrasonore et non par sa luminosité (C-scans au microscope acoustique à balayage, compromis sur la fréquence, POD): [explication](../../capabilities/sam-bond-voids.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_sam_hybrid_bonding_voids](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_sam_hybrid_bonding_voids.py)
- Localiser une pièce 3D connue dans un nuage de points avec six degrés de liberté: [explication](../../capabilities/six-dof-surface-pose.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_registration_basin](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_registration_basin.py) · [poc_real_tless_6dof](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_tless_6dof.py)
- Défauts de relief de surface, de la conception de l'éclairage à la détection: [explication](../../capabilities/surface-defects-with-lighting.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [illumination_design_demo](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/illumination_design_demo.py) · [photometric_stereo](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples_3d/photometric_stereo.py) · [poc_bump_coplanarity](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_bump_coplanarity.py) · [poc_surface_defect_classifiers](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_surface_defect_classifiers.py)
- Positionnement (suivi de la ligne de mesure par appariement de gabarit): [explication](../../capabilities/template-alignment.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_template_tracking](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_template_tracking.py) · [gallery2d_contour_measure](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/gallery2d_contour_measure.py) · [poc_search_sweep_width](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_search_sweep_width.py)

**Mesurer**

- Estimer la matrice intrinsèque K à partir de plusieurs vues d'une cible plane (méthode de Zhang): [explication](../../capabilities/camera-intrinsics-calibration.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [camera_intrinsics_calibration](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/camera_intrinsics_calibration.py) · [poc_caltab_chain_truth](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_caltab_chain_truth.py)
- Voir le plan complexe comme une « surface » (coloration de phase, bassins d'attraction, temps d'échappement, écoulement autour d'une aile): [explication](../../capabilities/complex-plane-fields.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_complex_plane_fields](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_complex_plane_fields.py)
- Mesurer en forme fermée la distance à laquelle un motif périodique se replie sur un réseau d'échantillonnage, et le temps de collision faussé par les franges (yeux composés et caméras d'inspection): [explication](../../capabilities/compound-eye-aliasing-and-ttc.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_zebra_stripes_fly_eye](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_zebra_stripes_fly_eye.py)
- Remesurer les valeurs de la fiche technique d'une caméra selon la procédure EMVA 1288 et observer dans quelle mesure le prétraitement interne à la caméra les déplace (Linear et General): [explication](../../capabilities/emva1288-camera-datasheets.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_emva1288_sensor](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_emva1288_sensor.py) · [poc_emva1288_in_camera_processing](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_emva1288_in_camera_processing.py)
- Estimer les coefficients de distorsion à partir de lignes censées être droites (plumb-line, sans damier): [explication](../../capabilities/estimate-lens-distortion.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [estimate_lens_distortion](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/estimate_lens_distortion.py)
- Ptychographie de Fourier (éclairage LED oblique, récupération de phase vers amplitude et phase à grande ouverture, refus sans convergence): [explication](../../capabilities/fourier-ptychography.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_fourier_ptychography](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_fourier_ptychography.py)
- Placer les mesures sur le globe terrestre (ECEF, références de hauteur, ENU local): [explication](../../capabilities/geodetic-frames.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_geodetic_height_frames](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_geodetic_height_frames.py) · [poc_geodetic_benchmarks_real](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_geodetic_benchmarks_real.py) · [dem_geodesy_tour](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/dem_geodesy_tour.py)
- Interférométrie à réseaux (Talbot / Talbot-Lau) : transmission, phase différentielle et champ sombre à partir d'un seul balayage par pas de phase: [explication](../../capabilities/grating-interferometry.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_grating_interferometry](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_grating_interferometry.py)
- OCT industrielle (spectre d'interférence → linéarisation en k → compensation de dispersion → A-scans / B-scans → épaisseur des couches par l'indice de groupe): [explication](../../capabilities/industrial-oct.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_industrial_oct](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_industrial_oct.py)
- Mesurer le diamètre intérieur d'une pièce circulaire jusqu'au mm: [explication](../../capabilities/inner-diameter-in-mm.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [example_inner_diameter_mm](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/example_inner_diameter_mm.py) · [poc_dimensional_inspection](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_dimensional_inspection.py) · [poc_real_coin_metrology](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_coin_metrology.py)
- Temps de vol indirect (par phase) : déroulement multifréquence et modes de défaillance (repliement, trajets multiples, pixels de bord mélangés, harmoniques, lumière ambiante): [explication](../../capabilities/itof-multifreq.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_itof_multifreq](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_itof_multifreq.py)
- Le plancher d'erreur de la coupe lumineuse laser est fixé par le speckle (imagerie cohérente, statistiques du speckle, incertitude de triangulation): [explication](../../capabilities/laser-speckle-floor.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_laser_line_speckle_floor](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_laser_line_speckle_floor.py)

- Valider une recette d'inspection et une spécification sur des jeux connus de pièces bonnes et défectueuses avant le déploiement, et mesurer les marges: [explication](../../capabilities/inspection-fixture.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [inspection_fixture](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/inspection_fixture.py)
- Inspecter un dossier en une seule fois, juger selon une spécification, puis produire agrégation, SPC, rapport et journal d'audit: [explication](../../capabilities/inspection-workflow.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [inspection_workflow](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/inspection_workflow.py)
- Budgéter les tolérances de fabrication d'un objectif selon la FTM et le rendement: [explication](../../capabilities/lens-tolerancing.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_lens_tolerancing](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_lens_tolerancing.py)
- Ne calculer qu'une fois la validation, les voisinages et les noyaux de transformation: [explication](../../capabilities/reuse-computation-results.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [perf_pointcloud_descriptors](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_pointcloud_descriptors.py) · [perf_monogenic_metrics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_monogenic_metrics.py) · [perf_prepared_volume_probe](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_prepared_volume_probe.py) · [perf_polarization_analyze](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_polarization_analyze.py) · [perf_hankel_plan_and_piv_stream](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/perf_hankel_plan_and_piv_stream.py)
- Lire des fichiers de données scientifiques (FITS, NIfTI, MRC, OME-TIFF, E57): [explication](../../capabilities/scientific-data-files.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_real_file_formats](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_file_formats.py)
- Écrire en JSON les valeurs de retour typées d'un op et les relire à l'identique bit à bit: [explication](../../capabilities/typed-results-as-json.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [typed_results_json](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/typed_results_json.py)
- Rendre lisibles en Markdown les valeurs de retour typées d'un op, en y intégrant du JSON pour la relecture: [explication](../../capabilities/typed-results-as-markdown.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [typed_results_markdown](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/typed_results_markdown.py)
- Exporter des résultats d'inspection typés dans un rapport Excel (.xlsx): [explication](../../capabilities/xlsx-report.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [xlsx_report](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/xlsx_report.py)

**Afficher**

- Transformer les résultats en figures lisibles par l'humain: [explication](../../capabilities/figures-and-annotation.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_colormap_readability](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_colormap_readability.py) · [poc_dem_terrain](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_dem_terrain.py)
- Transformer les résultats en une page autonome à manipuler (onglets, badges, 3-D, vidéo, langues): [explication](../../capabilities/interactive-poc-viewer.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [interactive_poc_viewer](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/interactive_poc_viewer.py) · [interactive_anim3d_viewer](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/interactive_anim3d_viewer.py)
- Tracer des lignes et des régions sans connaître la couleur du fond (couleur inversée): [explication](../../capabilities/inverted-colour-overlays.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [annotate_paper_tour](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/annotate_paper_tour.py)
- Dessiner directement un nuage de points en image couleur (empreintes sous-pixel, composition triée par profondeur, addition conservant la quantité de lumière): [explication](../../capabilities/point-cloud-to-picture.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [points_render_basics](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples_3d/points_render_basics.py) · [poc_flybrain_pointcloud](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_flybrain_pointcloud.py) · [poc_real_tless_6dof](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_real_tless_6dof.py)
- Placer du texte et des tableaux à l'endroit souhaité sur une image: [explication](../../capabilities/text-and-tables-on-images.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [annotate_paper_tour](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/annotate_paper_tour.py)

**Dessiner**

- Transformer une photographie en une seule ligne (pointillé, circuit, cercles en rotation): [explication](../../capabilities/one-stroke-drawing.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_one_stroke_epicycles](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_one_stroke_epicycles.py)
- Dessins de systèmes optiques (coupes de lentilles, schémas d'implantation de systèmes d'imagerie et dessins de fabrication de lentilles simples en SVG / DXF): [explication](../../capabilities/optics-drawings.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_optics_drawings](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_optics_drawings.py)
- Photographier une scène 3-D à travers la prescription réelle de l'objectif (profondeur de champ, bokeh en œil de chat, éclairement des coins, franges colorées, images fantômes): [explication](../../capabilities/photograph-through-prescription.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_photograph_through_prescription](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_photograph_through_prescription.py)
- Créer des images qui trompent l'œil et noter le système de mesure (illusions d'optique, dessin infini, vidéos en boucle): [explication](../../capabilities/pictures-that-carry-their-own-truth.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_illusions_and_perpetual_drawing](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_illusions_and_perpetual_drawing.py)
- Caméra plénoptique fondée sur une prescription optique: [explication](../../capabilities/plenoptic-camera.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_plenoptic_camera](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_plenoptic_camera.py)
- Des figures où un théorème fait barrière (Apollonius, Ford, dôme géodésique, phyllotaxie, IFS, courbes remplissant l'espace): [explication](../../capabilities/theorems-as-pictures.md) _(ja)_ · exemples [poc_theorems_as_pictures](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_theorems_as_pictures.py)

</div>

<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_illumination_cone_vs_na/06_hairline_cone_sweep.gif"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_illumination_cone_vs_na/02_works_by_cone.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_illumination_cone_vs_na/story.en.gif" alt="On near-mirror metal parts, shallow dents and scratches on a hairline finish show or vanish with the width of the cone of light more than with the lamp type. A single dent can appear or disappear as the cone opens." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>Cône d'éclairage et ON d'observation</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_tdi_linescan/01_tdi_stages_defects.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_tdi_linescan/01_tdi_stages_defects.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_tdi_linescan/story.en.gif" alt="Moving material such as film or steel strip is imaged with a line sensor. If transport and capture drift out of sync, the stacked image smears." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>Caméra linéaire TDI et objectifs pour caméra linéaire</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_industrial_oct/01_real_paint_bscans.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_industrial_oct/01_real_paint_bscans.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_industrial_oct/story.en.gif" alt="We want to see the layers of paint and multilayer film in depth without cutting them. Layers too thin to separate are reported as unmeasurable instead of guessed." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>OCT industrielle (épaisseur des couches de peinture et défauts internes)</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_fourier_ptychography/01_usaf_4x_fp_20x.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_fourier_ptychography/01_usaf_4x_fp_20x.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_fourier_ptychography/story.en.gif" alt="A low-magnification objective sees a wide field but misses fine detail. <a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_virtual_sem_kv_cd_bse/05_sem_same_part_3kv_3detectors.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_virtual_sem_kv_cd_bse/05_sem_same_part_3kv_3detectors.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_virtual_sem_kv_cd_bse/story.en.gif" alt="An SEM image is not a photo of the surface but a tally of electrons escaping after scattering inside the solid. <a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_grating_interferometry/12_real_three_images.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_grating_interferometry/12_real_three_images.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_grating_interferometry/story.en.gif" alt="Grating Interferometry — Fibre Orientation, Micro-Cracks and Pores That Absorption Cannot See, Read From the Fringe Amplitude (Dark Field)" loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>Interférométrie à réseaux (fibres, fissures et pores en champ sombre)</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_dual_energy_xray/13_real_tray_xray.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_dual_energy_xray/13_real_tray_xray.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_dual_energy_xray/story.en.gif" alt="In a food X-ray image, thick meat and a thin hard fragment can look the same. The material itself could not be read for a thin wire or under thick meat." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>Trouver os et verre dans la viande avec deux énergies de rayons X (rayons X bi-énergie)</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_code_matching_breakdown/05_highlight_sweep.gif"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_code_matching_breakdown/01_breakdown_occlusion.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_code_matching_breakdown/story.en.gif" alt="Normalised cross-correlation, the usual template matcher, fails when part of the target is hidden or glare washes it out. They withstand occlusion far better, yet every method stays weak to rotation." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>Appariement de codes sous lumière variable et occlusion</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_good_sample_variation_model/01_band_width_alignment.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_good_sample_variation_model/01_band_width_alignment.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_good_sample_variation_model/story.en.gif" alt="A classic inspection learns the usual per-pixel variation from good parts and flags what falls outside. Alignment mattered more than the number of good parts, and the declared rate was not kept." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>Modèle de variation des pièces bonnes</span></a>

With identical prints, deep grooves favour colour; only wide, untaped cuts over shallow grooves favour depth." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><b>&#9654;</b><span>Cartons mélangés : marche de profondeur, rainure en V ou couleur</span></a>

Cloud LLMs beat chance but did not reach the existing recipes." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>Noter sur données réelles des inspections construites par une IA</span></a>

It is a synthetic test, not the performance of real equipment." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>Carte de choix du matériel : les mêmes défauts vus par 60 combinaisons éclairage, caméra, objectif</span></a>

<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_surface_defect_classifiers/01_defect_patches.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_surface_defect_classifiers/01_defect_patches.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_surface_defect_classifiers/story.en.gif" alt="Small pieces of ground metal were drawn with scratches, dents and stains by physics, and sorted with trained classifiers. Flaws that run with the grinding marks are easily missed, and unknown holes are rejected." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>Trier les défauts de surface métallique par classifieurs appris : les rayures le long des stries de meulage sont manquées trois fois sur quatre, les piqûres inconnues rejetées à 95 %</span></a>

<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_aoi_ct_traceability/01_inspection_and_reconstructed_ct.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_aoi_ct_traceability/01_inspection_and_reconstructed_ct.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_aoi_ct_traceability/story.en.gif" alt="Full visual inspection and sampled CT must assign the same index to each joint. Unequal-sided fiducials fixed this correspondence, then top-ranked candidates caught 19 % of interface-touching voids. Apparent void rankings can agree while most life-limiting parts are missed." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>Appariement AOI et CT</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_asbuilt_wall_deviation/01_scene.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_asbuilt_wall_deviation/01_scene.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_asbuilt_wall_deviation/story.en.gif" alt="Point-cloud dimensions can confuse room heading or furniture with wall position. A room with known tilt and bulges was rotated, while its plane-pair error stayed at +2.24 mm. Bounding boxes grow with heading and sample count; plane fitting jumps to furniture when outliers become the majority." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>Déformation des murs construits</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_battery_electrode_breathing/01_scene.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_battery_electrode_breathing/01_scene.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_battery_electrode_breathing/story.en.gif" alt="Slight electrode growth must be separated from brightness changes between scans. Layer boundaries were tracked across two cross-sections, recovering a stack increase of +2.136 µm. A fixed brightness crossing reports false strain after an offset, whereas the gradient peak remains at zero." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>Respiration des électrodes</span></a>
<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_bilateral_asymmetry/01_floor_vs_spacing.png"><picture><source media="(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)" srcset="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_bilateral_asymmetry/01_floor_vs_spacing.png"><img src="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_bilateral_asymmetry/story.en.gif" alt="A reference plane for left-right asymmetry may be pulled toward a one-sided deformation. A known bulge was added to a symmetric skull; the best-fit plane moved by 2.92 mm. The optimized plane shifts toward the bulge and removes part of the asymmetry that should be reported." loading="lazy" width="200" height="200"></picture><span>Asymétrie bilatérale</span></a>

<a href="../../articles/assets/poc/poc_interferometry_step/09_csi_scan_explained.mp4"><img src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAAAAACH5BAEKAAEALAAAAAABAAEAAAICTAEAOw==" data-src="../thumbs/all/poc_interferometry_step.jpg" alt="Marches en interférométrie en lumière blanche" width="200" height="200"><b>&#9654;</b><span>Marches en interférométrie en lumière blanche</span></a>

## Ce que l'on voit et la valeur mesurée

Chaque valeur est mesurée par rapport à la vérité terrain que chaque PoC a elle-même définie (forme close, solution analytique ou valeur publiée) ; l'exécution du PoC affiche la même valeur.

<div class="vl" markdown="1">

**Inspection d'images et mesure d'aspect**

- [Grandissement vérifié à 0.05 % sur des images télécentriques publiques](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_telecentric_metrology/08_real_alignment_plate.png) (figure): Measured in one image, a stepped part looks larger where it is nearer, so an ordinary lens misreads its dimensions. On the published real images from a telecentric lens, the magnification matched the paper within 0.05 %. In the synthetic chapter, telecentric magnification barely moves with object height.
  The lead chapter measures the paper's raw telecentric images (Stark et al., Sci. Rep. 15, 28690, 2025, CC BY 4.0) with Fullseye's metrology ops and checks them against the paper: a family of ellipses sharing one centre and shape, fitted to the eight rings of an alignment plate (1 mm spacing) tilted by 29.5°, gives 252.13 ± 0.04 px/mm along the major axis, +0.05 % from the paper's K1 = 252.0 px/mm (the paper's own K1 and K2 spread by ±0.25 %, so the match is not read as finer than that). The telecentric signature: the centres of the concentric rings on the tilted plate do not drift with r² (β = 0.0035 ± 0.0034 px/mm², where a 25 mm entocentric lens would give 1.40 and move the outer centre by 101 px), so the entrance pupil lies beyond 10.5 m. Fitting a free ellipse to each ring instead fails on the outer rings whose arcs are shadowed down to 36–180°. A 3D-printed resin sphere (nominal radius 2.5 mm) is not a usable reference: its outline radius moves 8 % with the threshold. The synthetic chapters that follow sweep the failure conditions. Die bonding and connector-pin inspection measure features at different heights in one image. An ordinary (entocentric) lens magnifies nearer faces more, so the same dimension reads differently above and below a step; a telecentric lens puts the stop at a focal point so the chief rays run parallel to the axis. Seven new ops (`telecentric`, category `telecentric` of `opsoptics`): the same BK7-class singlet (f = 100 mm) with only the stop moved (entocentric, object-side and bi-telecentric), the real chief ray found by aiming at the stop itself so it works with the entrance pupil at infinity (where `raytrace.chief_ray` stops), magnification versus height, the error across a step, defocus blur, vignetting and a pinhole camera at the entrance pupil for `render3d`. Moving the object 5 mm closer on a fixed image plane changes the magnification by +6.9 % for a 25 mm machine-vision lens at 75 mm, +1.7 % for the 100 mm entocentric lens and −0.013 % for the telecentric ones; the real rays agree with L/(L − Δz) to 7e-5, and refocusing does not help (m = −f/(s − f) moves by +2.6 %). Rendered through the three lenses, a 5 mm pin top reads 6.9 % too large with the short lens (closed form to 7e-4) and its reading moves 8.6 % over a 6 mm stroke; 2,053 pixels of the bore wall show and only 0.948 of the hole bottom is visible (circle-overlap closed form 0.949), while the bi-telecentric view sees the whole bottom and no wall. The parts are rendered with real material appearance (a brushed aluminium fixture plate, a gold-plated pin, a black moulded package with tinned leads; machining marks and metal colours from complex refractive indices, a clear-coat resin, shadows and ambient occlusion), and under coaxial, dome and low-angle ring lighting a circle metrology model applied to the rendered image reads the pin top within 0.33 % of the closed form, +8.3 to 8.4 % across a 6 mm stroke for the short lens and within ±0.16 % for the bi-telecentric one; at a coarser 47 µm per pixel the low-angle ring reading drifts by 2 % because the dark-field edge is weak. What remains: the singlet's pupil spherical aberration leaves 0.05° at 8 mm field (growing as h³) and 3 % distortion for the object-side design (0.005 % bi-telecentric); a stop shift moves the entrance pupil to −f²/δ as Newton's equation says (0.04 % at 0.5 mm field, 11.8 % more at 8 mm); a residual 0.1° shifts a point 9.2 µm across a 5 mm step (closed form 8.7 µm). The front lens sets the field: the 50 % point at 11.6 mm falls 0.8 mm short of the paraxial overlap because pupil aberration shifts and widens the bundle. The blur slope matches |m|·NA/√2 within 0.5 %, and NA 0.033 (the smallest that resolves 22 µm) caps the depth of field at 0.68 mm.  **A second implementation, the open-source optiland, gives the same chief-ray image height to 9e-8 mm (with the same index from a public glass catalogue, so it checks the tracing and aiming code). Nothing learned, nothing calibrated, 29 gates.** [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_telecentric_metrology.py)
- [Défauts d'après la variabilité des bonnes pièces : le taux de fausses alarmes annoncé ne tient pas sur images réelles](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_good_sample_variation_model/04_visa_explanation.png) (figure): A classic inspection learns the usual per-pixel variation from good parts and flags what falls outside. Here the band is set by a false-alarm rate declared in advance and scored on 200 public real images. Alignment mattered more than the number of good parts, and the declared rate was not kept.
  The inspection classic, learning every pixel's usual value and spread from many good samples and flagging what leaves the band, as an op family whose band width is derived from a declared per-pixel false-alarm rate instead of being set by hand (five new varmodel_* ops plus four method-independent anomeval metrics). For normal good samples a new pixel's deviation follows a t prediction interval, so the band width is closed-form. False-alarm rates counted on synthetic good samples fall inside the binomial 4-sigma band for per-pixel std, one pooled variance, empirical-Bayes shrinkage (Smyth 2004) and median/MAD, and the pooled variance fails when the noise differs per pixel. With five good samples the std band is 9.4 sigma wide (the t4 tail); shrinkage brings it to 5.0 and lifts the detection of 5-sigma flaws from 0.13 to 0.52. A good set shifted by 1-2 px is refused; forced through, the band on edges grows 22-fold and misses every edge flaw, and registering by a 1/20-px Fourier shift brings it back to 1.07. With three of twenty "good" samples carrying the same flaw, std misses the real flaw there (0.00) while median/MAD finds it (0.98). On the public VisA pcb1 data (CC BY 4.0, 200 test images) the registered band reaches pixel AUROC 0.808 and AUPRO 0.678 against 0.705 and 0.460 unregistered; golden comparison scores a higher pixel AUROC (0.826) but a lower AUPRO (0.519).  **The declared 1e-3 false-alarm rate is not kept on real images (3.8 %), and recalibrating on held-out good images only reaches 2.4 %: a difference between the training and test captures remains that a translation cannot remove. A stochastic texture (DAGM 2007, CC BY 4.0) has no structure in its mean image, its alignment is reported as unobservable and detection is at chance (pixel AUROC 0.465): where the method works and where it does not, both in numbers.** [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_good_sample_variation_model.py)
- [Trier les défauts de surface métallique par classifieurs appris : les rayures le long des stries de meulage sont manquées trois fois sur quatre, les piqûres inconnues rejetées à 95 %](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_surface_defect_classifiers/01_defect_patches.png) (figure): Small pieces of ground metal were drawn with scratches, dents and stains by physics, and sorted with trained classifiers. Stained pixels were painted with an overlap of 0.915 by a classifier learned from normal pixels. Flaws that run with the grinding marks are easily missed, and unknown holes are rejected.
  Ground-metal patches (96x96 px) are rendered with scratches (V-grooves), dents (smooth pits) and stains (a brown drop in reflectance). Height defects change only the shading and the stain changes only the colour, with a defect weight from 0 to 1. At pixel level, learn_ndim_norm learns a normal model from clean metal pixels and class_ndim_norm paints the anomalous ones. Stain pixels reach IoU 0.915 with classify_image_class_lut, 0.729 with class_2dim_sup and 0.535 with class_2dim_unsup on standardised channels. Seven region features train GMM, MLP, SVM and k-NN classifiers. Over five seeds their accuracy is 0.871-0.886 (±0.02). Each stays within 0.41 of the tolerance 3·sqrt(2p(1−p)/n) of a scikit-learn second implementation, and the one-component GMM agrees 100 % with closed-form QDA. Corrosion pits, never trained, are 94.8 % rejected by a log-density threshold set on the validation split; 6.0 % of known patches are rejected. Permuted labels drop the accuracy to 0.238. The stain's 100 % recall holds because it is the only defect that changes colour, not because a feature leaks the truth. Scratches within 15° of the grinding lay are recalled at 0.275, against 0.949 above 45°: the gradient chosen to suppress the lay suppresses scratches along it too. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_surface_defect_classifiers.py)
- [Noter sur données réelles des inspections construites par une IA](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_ai_built_inspection_scored/01_crack_real.png) (figure): We gave an AI a task in natural language and let it assemble the inspection recipe. It was scored on 400 public crack images against the labels from their source, and each failure was sorted into tool, MCP or model. Cloud LLMs beat chance but did not reach the existing recipes.
  A task is given in natural language and an LLM uses only Fullseye's MCP server (minimal profile) to find operators (index search), check the type chain and, as instructed, try candidates on six development images before returning a recipe (an operator chain), scored against the ground truth of public real data. Only a local generator script calls the LLM; every recipe, including runs that returned none, is saved as JSON with a pinned fingerprint and replayed deterministically through the same entry point as MCP. Tasks: cracks in concrete walls (Özgenel 2019, CC BY 4.0; 400 test images with the provider's labels) and the VisA candle set (CC BY 4.0; 197 test images with pixel masks). A recipe 'sorts' when the lower end of the 95% interval of its balanced accuracy is above 0.5 and it does not flag 95% or more of the images; this is beating chance, not a usable inspection. How the runs were redone (found by a review independent of the author): batch 1 sent no context length and the server default of 4,096 tokens very likely dropped the task text. The development images were read in 1 of 40 runs in batch 1, 40 of 40 in batch 2 (context 32,768) and 39 of 40 in batch 3, after fixing the MCP server (task files listed, missing names refused with readable names, out-of-range knobs rejected by the validator, a fix for knobs inside params, crack and stain queries reaching bothat and hx_plane_deviation). Results (local 14B and 32B code-tuned models, ten runs each per task): recipes that sort cracks: 1, 3 and 1 of 20 in batches 1-3 (0 to 2 per model and batch), so the local models rarely succeed and do not repeat it. The best of them on cracks, a batch-2 14B recipe (0.995 detected, 0.45 false alarms, balanced accuracy 0.773), beats every cloud run; none sort candles in any batch. Most batch 2 and 3 recipes were edge detectors flagging 95% or more of the images (26 of 40 and 31 of 40); without the rule that excludes them, batch-3 crack counts would be 32B 1/10, 14B 4/10. The MCP fixes did not raise success, and from batch 2 on almost every failure is attributed to the model (batch 1 failures were all attributed to the tool; the MCP rule alone would claim 23 of its 39 failures). A cloud LLM agent (separate column, MCP before the fixes) had the same MCP, task text, system prompt, no pixels and a 24-call cap (the local tool counted calls only at the start of a turn and ran up to 33; the 10 runs over the cap are scored with the recipe cut at call 24). Not matched: the local runs get one nudge each for answering without a trial or without JSON, the cloud model's product name is deliberately not written and its temperature is not exposed by the general-purpose coding agent that ran it, and all six cloud runs cut or parsed the printed responses with their own commands (a breach of the brief; the local models read the whole truncated response). All three cloud runs sort cracks, but with 0.32-0.52 false alarms, i.e. only above chance (best 0.81 detected, 0.32 false alarms); one of three sorts candles (0.26, 0.12). References: on cracks, an existing recipe (the hairline-scratch recipe applied unchanged) detects 0.69 with 0.01 false alarms, above the best cloud run. The developer-built recipe (by the cloud LLM that develops this PoC, knowing the internals, from the same six images with no trial limit; not a human) detects 0.98 with 0.08 false alarms and the learned-from-good baseline (memory bank, 20 good images to learn, 10 others for the threshold; not callable through MCP) 0.98 and 0.18. On candles the developer recipe also stays at chance (0.63, 0.73: the wick hole cannot be excluded and the per-image normalisation of the black top-hat reverses the area ranking); the learned baseline gives 0.53 and 0.13. Model names are generic and the LLM endpoint is never recorded. A 72B model was stopped after its first request did not return within 600 s. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_ai_built_inspection_scored.py)
  Terms: MCP — An open protocol through which an AI application calls external tools and data: it hands the AI each tool's name, argument schema and results.; LLM — A large neural network trained on text that writes prose or code on request; a smaller one run on one's own computer is called a local LLM.
- [Relire une bouteille en verre à partir de rayures d'arrière-plan (simulation) : reconstruction sous hypothèses partagées, ce qui ne se sépare ni ne se détecte](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_bottle_pattern_inverse/01_checker_through_bottle.png) (figure): A transparent bottle distorts the stripes behind it. For each pixel we find where the background is seen, fit the bottle geometry and indices backwards, and score 5 fill states against the true values of the simulation. Quantities that are inseparable and errors that stay undetectable are reported too.
  A transparent bottle distorts the stripes behind it. For every pixel the map u says where on the background the ray lands, and radius, wall, n_glass, n_liquid and the fill line are fitted back from it (all in simulation). Two new ops (`watervision`, category `watervision` of `opsoptics`): `bottle_background_map` (the same bottle and camera as `bottle_image`, returning the background coordinate u instead of a brightness) and `bottle_fit_from_pattern` (a fit of the u map returning standard deviations, the correlation matrix, a condition number, the weakest direction and a `separated` flag). Disclosure of the truth and of the shared assumptions: the truth is the input of a simulation made here (R 30 mm, wall 1.5 mm, n_glass 1.52, water 1.333 or oil 1.47, five fills), not a measurement of a real bottle. The observation (a vector-Snell trace) and the forward model of the fit (a closed form on concentric circles) are separate derivations, but both assume the same world: concentric circles with a uniform wall, a horizontal 2-D section, an ideal pinhole, a perfectly flat background, exactly known distances and pixel pitch, no dispersion, a flat liquid surface and point sampling. The result is a recovery under the same assumptions, not evidence that a real bottle can be measured. A paraxial 2x2 ray matrix checks the centre slope (2.2e-5). (1) Without noise all 10 configurations come back to a relative 9.5e-13 and the fill line is exactly on the row boundary; an empty and a full bottle return no fill line. (2) Four-step stripes of periods 256, 32 and 4 mm, shot at 8 bit with 1.5 grey levels of noise, give u back (noise 7.1 um = 0.11 px): R within 1.4e-6, n_liquid within 2.8e-6, fill line 0 px. With 300 noise trials on one row the worst case is 1.8e-5 for R and 1.7e-5 for n_liquid; the sigma predicted from the covariance is 0.67 to 1.00 of the empirical one, so it leans towards an underestimate. (3) The two readings of the fill line (the jump between rows of the stripe map and the backlit brightness) agree to 0 px, but they share the same row split, so this is not an independent measurement; with a 3 mm meniscus the two brightness readings split by 1.15 mm, and the stripe side has no meniscus, so it is unchecked. (4) What cannot be separated: wall and n_glass correlate at -0.92 (cond 11). With the edge pixels even a 0.1 mm wall comes back, but with the usable window narrowed to the middle 25 % the relative sigma of the wall grows 800 times and a 0.1 mm wall does not come back (0.05). The `separated` flag is a rule of thumb for noise-only error, not a guarantee: its threshold was set to 1 % after looking at 1600 trials (at 0.25 it missed 15-20 % of the wrong wall estimates and 73-76 % of the trials with n_glass off by 0.02; at 1 % it missed 0 of the 92 wrong trials of this sweep). With the pattern distance unknown its correlation with n_liquid is -0.999. (5) Precision is not accuracy: noise alone gives n_glass a sigma of 3e-5, but a 1 mm error of the pattern distance moves it by 0.011 (355 times) and a 1 mm error of the camera distance by 0.0029. (6) An error that cannot be detected: an inner wall shifted 0.3 mm (20 % of the wall) along the line of sight in a full bottle (an independently written scalar trace) moves n_glass by -0.095 while the residual stays at the noise floor and converged and separated stay true; in a half-filled bottle the residual grows 8 times and gives it away, but the flag knows nothing of eccentricity. (7) Break-it probes: fixing n_glass at 1.4 raises the residual 414 times and reversing Snell's law 80,000 times. Vertical refraction is not modelled. No learning, no calibration (distances are known), 37 gates. [source](https://github.com/furuse-kazufumi/fullseye/blob/master/examples/poc_bottle_pattern_inverse.py)
- [CT mesuré : ignorer le décalage vertical éloigne 2,6 fois de la référence, mais le résidu ne le voit pas](../../articles/assets/poc/poc_real_walnut_cbct/07_geometry_mistakes.png) (figure): A walnut was really scanned in a cone beam, and the data were reconstructed with FDK and iterative methods. Using 300 projections and scoring against the reference from its authors, the result that ignores the vertical shift ends up much more distant. But the residual after reprojection cannot reveal the gap.
  A publicly released, measured cone-beam CT scan (Der Sarkissian et al. 2019, one walnut, CC BY 4.0) is reconstructed with Fullseye's scan-mode operators, comparing FDK and three iterative methods (SIRT, OS-SART and total variation, TV) against the reference the providers built from all three of their orbits. It uses 300 of the 1200 projections of the middle orbit (1.2 degree steps), binned 6x6 for the mid-plane and 8x8 for 3-D; a fetch tool pulls only the needed members out of a 6 GB zip with HTTP Range requests (1309 members, pinned by a SHA-256 list). Geometry: read as the circular orbit of `ct_scan_geometry`, the providers' 300 vectors give the SOD and SDD of the scanner's settings file; the two things a circular orbit cannot express (a 0.50 degree in-plane detector roll and a 7.2 pixel vertical offset) are removed by resampling the projections with `warp_parametric`. Mid-plane: FDK on all 300 views scores RMSE 0.0023 /mm and SSIM 0.94 against the reference, and SSIM 0.96 with mean ratio 0.9988 against the providers' FDK (ASTRA, 1200 views). Most of the gap to that FDK's 0.0009 is the coarse grid: rebuilt at 0.1 mm and box-averaged it is 0.0013. At 25 views FDK, SIRT, OS-SART and TV give 0.0126, 0.0074, 0.0067 and 0.0046: TV holds up best, with its weight 1e-05 chosen without the reference, by the reprojection error on views it did not use. With a 90 degree range every method is over four times worse than 300-view FDK (TV 0.0110); TV fills the missing directions with flat patches whose shapes differ from the reference. 3-D: Fullseye's FDK and ASTRA's FDK correlate at 0.9946, and both degrade the same way towards the lower end (0.0026 to 0.0101 for Fullseye, 0.0008 to 0.0099 for ASTRA): a circular orbit does not fully measure the planes away from the mid-plane (Tuy's condition). Geometry mistakes: the adjoint identity <Ax,y> = <x,A^T y> holds to 1e-15 on this scanner's geometry, but it only says the projector pair is matched (mismatching the geometries of A and A^T gives 1.1). Ignoring the vertical offset worsens the RMSE to 0.0060, yet the reprojection residual drops to 0.0850 from 0.0870: a blind spot of the residual. A 0.3 mm lateral shift gives 0.0096 and a reversed rotation 0.0209; the 0.5 degree roll barely matters on a 0.3 mm mid-plane grid. Dual energy: the data hold one energy, so the second energy is a closed-form synthesis, not measured data.
